00460 |       const hasValidExtraPrice = extraUnitPrice !== null;
00461 | 
00462 |       const unitPrice = (channelPrice ?? 0) + (extraUnitPrice ?? 0);
00463 |       const lineTotal =
00464 |         hasValidBasePrice && hasValidExtraPrice ? unitPrice * item.quantity : 0;
00465 | 
00466 |       acc.subtotal += lineTotal;
00467 | 
00468 |       if (item.isGift) {
00469 |         acc.giftDiscountTotal += lineTotal;
00470 |       }
00471 | 
00472 |       if (!item.isGift && potSkus.has(item.product.sku)) {
00473 |         acc.potQuantity += item.quantity;
00474 | 
00475 |         const rewardAmountForLine =
00476 |           selectedReward && item.product.id === selectedReward.rewardProductId
00477 |             ? unitPrice
00478 |             : 0;
00479 | 
00480 |         acc.potSubtotal += Math.max(0, lineTotal - rewardAmountForLine);
00481 |       }
00482 | 
00483 |       return acc;
00484 |     },
00485 |     {
00486 |       subtotal: 0,
00487 |       potQuantity: 0,
00488 |       potSubtotal: 0,
00489 |       giftDiscountTotal: 0,
00490 |     },
00491 |   );
00492 | 
00493 |   const discountRate =
00494 |     channel === "local"
00495 |       ? pricing.potQuantity >= 6
00496 |         ? 0.15
00497 |         : pricing.potQuantity >= 4
00498 |           ? 0.1
00499 |           : 0
00500 |       : channel === "shopify"
00501 |         ? pricing.potQuantity >= 9
00502 |           ? 0.15
00503 |           : pricing.potQuantity >= 4 && pricing.potQuantity !== 6
00504 |             ? 0.1
00505 |             : 0
00506 |         : 0;
00507 | 
00508 |   const mysteryRewardDiscountTotal =
00509 |     channel === "shopify" && pricing.potQuantity >= 7
00510 |       ? products.find((product) => product.sku === "POT-16-LISTO")
00511 |         ? (getChannelPrice(
00512 |             products.find((product) => product.sku === "POT-16-LISTO")!,
00513 |           ) ?? 0)
00514 |         : 0
00515 |       : 0;
00516 | 
00517 |   const potDiscountBase =
00518 |     channel === "shopify" && pricing.potQuantity >= 7
00519 |       ? Math.max(0, pricing.potSubtotal - mysteryRewardDiscountTotal)
00520 |       : pricing.potSubtotal;
00521 | 
00522 |   const potDiscountTotal = Math.round(potDiscountBase * discountRate);
00523 | 
00524 |   const totalBeforeManualDiscount = Math.max(
00525 |     0,
00526 |     pricing.subtotal -
00527 |       pricing.giftDiscountTotal -
00528 |       mysteryRewardDiscountTotal -
00529 |       rewardDiscountTotal -
00530 |       potDiscountTotal,
00531 |   );
00532 | 
00533 |   const parsedManualDiscountValue = Number(manualDiscountValue);
00534 | 
00535 |   const manualDiscountAmount = manualDiscountEnabled
00536 |     ? manualDiscountType === "percent"
00537 |       ? Math.min(
00538 |           totalBeforeManualDiscount,
00539 |           Math.round(
00540 |             (totalBeforeManualDiscount * parsedManualDiscountValue) / 100,
00541 |           ),
00542 |         )
00543 |       : Math.min(totalBeforeManualDiscount, parsedManualDiscountValue)
00544 |     : 0;
00545 | 
00546 |   const discountTotal =
00547 |     potDiscountTotal +
00548 |     pricing.giftDiscountTotal +
00549 |     mysteryRewardDiscountTotal +
00550 |     rewardDiscountTotal +
00551 |     (Number.isFinite(manualDiscountAmount) ? manualDiscountAmount : 0);
00552 | 
00553 |   const total = Math.max(0, pricing.subtotal - discountTotal);
00554 | 
00555 |   function handleClienteChange(cliente: ClienteSelectorValue | null) {
00556 |     setSelectedCliente(cliente);
00557 |     setSelectedRewardId(null);
00558 |   }
00559 | 
00560 |   function handleRewardChange(rewardId: number | null) {
00561 |     setSelectedRewardId(rewardId);
00562 |   }
00563 | 
00564 |   function handlePaymentMethodChange(value: string) {
00565 |     setPaymentMethod(value);
00566 |     setCashReceived("");
00567 |   }
00568 | 
00569 |   function validarVenta() {
00570 |     if (cart.length === 0) {
00571 |       return "Agrega al menos una línea a la venta.";
00572 |     }
00573 | 
00574 |     if (channel === "shopify" && pricing.potQuantity === 6) {
00575 |       return "Un pedido Shopify de 6 potes no es válido: desde 6 potes pagados, Shopify incorpora un séptimo pote como Premio Misterioso.";
00576 |     }
00577 | 
00578 |     const unavailableItem = cart.find(
00579 |       (item) => item.itemType === "product" && !isProductSellable(item.product),
00580 |     );
00581 | 
00582 |     if (unavailableItem?.itemType === "product") {
00583 |       return `${unavailableItem.product.name} no está disponible para el canal seleccionado o no tiene una configuración comercial válida.`;
00584 |     }
00585 | 
00586 |     const unavailableCoffeeItem = cart.find(
00587 |       (item): item is ProductCartItem =>
00588 |         item.itemType === "product" &&
00589 |         item.product.sku === "CAFE" &&
00590 |         getItemExtraUnitPrice(item) === null,
00591 |     );
00592 | 
00593 |     if (unavailableCoffeeItem) {
00594 |       return "El tipo de café seleccionado no tiene un precio disponible para el canal actual.";
00595 |     }
00596 | 
00597 |     if (selectedRewardId !== null && !selectedReward) {
00598 |       return "El premio seleccionado ya no está disponible para esta venta.";
00599 |     }
00600 | 
00601 |     if (channel !== "local" && !externalOrderId.trim()) {
00602 |       return "Ingresa el número externo del pedido digital.";
00603 |     }
00604 | 
00605 |     if (manualDiscountEnabled) {
00606 |       if (
00607 |         manualDiscountValue.trim() === "" ||
00608 |         !Number.isInteger(parsedManualDiscountValue) ||
00609 |         parsedManualDiscountValue <= 0
00610 |       ) {
00611 |         return "Ingresa un valor válido para el descuento manual.";
00612 |       }
00613 | 
00614 |       if (manualDiscountType === "percent" && parsedManualDiscountValue > 100) {
00615 |         return "El descuento porcentual no puede superar 100%.";
00616 |       }
00617 | 
00618 |       if (
00619 |         manualDiscountType === "fixed" &&
00620 |         parsedManualDiscountValue > totalBeforeManualDiscount
00621 |       ) {
00622 |         return "El descuento manual no puede superar el total vigente.";
00623 |       }
00624 | 
00625 |       if (!manualDiscountReason) {
00626 |         return "Selecciona el motivo del descuento manual.";
00627 |       }
00628 | 
00629 |       if (manualDiscountReason === "other" && !manualDiscountNotes.trim()) {
00630 |         return "Especifica el motivo del descuento manual.";
00631 |       }
00632 |     }
00633 | 
00634 |     if (paymentMethod === "efectivo") {
00635 |       const receivedAmount = Number(cashReceived);
00636 | 
00637 |       if (
00638 |         cashReceived.trim() === "" ||
00639 |         !Number.isFinite(receivedAmount) ||
00640 |         receivedAmount < total
00641 |       ) {
00642 |         return "El monto recibido es inferior al total.";
00643 |       }
00644 |     }
00645 | 
00646 |     if (
00647 |       !Number.isInteger(promotionalStamps) ||
00648 |       promotionalStamps < 0 ||
00649 |       promotionalStamps > 5
00650 |     ) {
00651 |       return "La cantidad de sellos promocionales no es válida.";
00652 |     }
00653 | 
00654 |     if (promotionalStamps > 0 && !selectedCliente) {
00655 |       return "Selecciona un cliente para asignar sellos promocionales.";
00656 |     }
00657 | 
00658 |     for (const item of cart) {
00659 |       if (item.itemType === "custom") {
00660 |         if (!item.customName.trim()) {
00661 |           return "El ítem personalizado debe tener un nombre.";
00662 |         }
00663 | 
00664 |         if (
00665 |           !Number.isInteger(item.customUnitPrice) ||
00666 |           item.customUnitPrice <= 0
00667 |         ) {
00668 |           return `El precio de ${item.customName} no es válido.`;
00669 |         }
00670 | 
00671 |         if (!Number.isInteger(item.quantity) || item.quantity <= 0) {
00672 |           return `La cantidad de ${item.customName} no es válida.`;
00673 |         }
00674 | 
00675 |         continue;
00676 |       }
00677 |       if (item.isGift) {
00678 |         const giftReason = item.giftReason?.trim() || "";
00679 | 
00680 |         if (!giftReason) {
00681 |           return `Debes indicar el motivo del regalo para ${item.product.name}.`;
00682 |         }
00683 | 
00684 |         if (
00685 |           giftReason === "Otro" ||
00686 |           giftReason === "Otro:" ||
00687 |           (giftReason.startsWith("Otro:") &&
00688 |             !giftReason.slice("Otro:".length).trim())
00689 |         ) {
00690 |           return `Debes especificar el motivo del regalo para ${item.product.name}.`;
00691 |         }
00692 |       }
00693 | 
00694 |       if (item.isGift && item.giftReason?.trim() === "Otro") {
00695 |         return `Debes especificar el motivo del regalo para ${item.product.name}.`;
00696 |       }
00697 |       const isServedIceCream =
00698 |         item.product.category?.trim().toLowerCase() === "helados" &&
00699 |         item.product.operational_type?.trim().toLowerCase() === "servido";
00700 | 
00701 |       /*
00702 |        * Los helados servidos no requieren sabor en caja.
00703 |        * Los potes armados y demás productos configurables sí.
00704 |        */
00705 |       const requiresBrownieVariety =
00706 |         item.product.sku === "BROWNIE" || item.product.sku === "BROWNIE-HELADO";
00707 | 
00708 |       if (
00709 |         requiresBrownieVariety &&
00710 |         (!Number.isInteger(item.brownieVarietyId) ||
00711 |           Number(item.brownieVarietyId) <= 0)
00712 |       ) {
00713 |         return `Debes seleccionar una variedad para ${item.product.name}.`;
00714 |       }
00715 | 
00716 |       if (
00717 |         !isServedIceCream &&
00718 |         item.product.has_flavors &&
00719 |         item.product.sku !== "POT-16-ARMADO" &&
00720 |         item.flavorSelections.length === 0
00721 |       ) {
00722 |         return `Debes seleccionar sabor para ${item.product.name}.`;
00723 |       }
00724 | 
00725 |       if (
00726 |         !isServedIceCream &&
00727 |         item.product.has_flavors &&
00728 |         item.flavorSelections.length > item.product.max_flavors
00729 |       ) {
00730 |         return `${item.product.name} supera el máximo de sabores.`;
00731 |       }
00732 | 
00733 |       if (
00734 |         item.product.allows_toppings &&
00735 |         item.toppingIds.length > item.product.max_toppings
00736 |       ) {
00737 |         return `${item.product.name} supera el máximo de toppings.`;
00738 |       }
00739 | 
00740 |       const requiresMineralWaterType =
00741 |         item.product.sku === "AGUA-MINERAL-500CC";
00742 | 
00743 |       if (
00744 |         requiresMineralWaterType &&
00745 |         (!Number.isInteger(item.mineralWaterTypeId) ||
00746 |           Number(item.mineralWaterTypeId) <= 0)
00747 |       ) {
00748 |         return `Debes seleccionar el tipo para ${item.product.name}.`;
00749 |       }
00750 | 
00751 |       const requiresCoffeeType = item.product.sku === "CAFE";
00752 | 
00753 |       if (
00754 |         requiresCoffeeType &&
00755 |         (!Number.isInteger(item.coffeeTypeId) || Number(item.coffeeTypeId) <= 0)
00756 |       ) {
00757 |         return `Debes seleccionar el tipo de café para ${item.product.name}.`;
00758 |       }
00759 |     }
00760 | 
00761 |     return null;
00762 |   }
00763 | 
00764 |   function getCartItemSignature(item: Omit<ProductCartItem, "localId">) {
00765 |     return JSON.stringify({
00766 |       productId: item.product.id,
00767 | 
00768 |       flavorSelections: [...(item.flavorSelections || [])].sort(
00769 |         (a, b) => a - b,
00770 |       ),
00771 | 
00772 |       toppingIds: [...(item.toppingIds || [])].sort((a, b) => a - b),
00773 | 
00774 |       brownieVarietyId: item.brownieVarietyId ?? null,
00775 | 
00776 |       serviceFormat: item.serviceFormat || null,
00777 | 
00778 |       includesCookie: Boolean(item.includesCookie),
00779 | 
00780 |       chocolateDip: Boolean(item.chocolateDip),
00781 | 
00782 |       extraToppingSelections: [...(item.extraToppingSelections || [])].sort(
00783 |         (a, b) => a - b,
00784 |       ),
00785 | 
00786 |       notes: item.notes?.trim() || "",
00787 | 
00788 |       extraUnitPrice: item.extraUnitPrice || 0,
00789 | 
00790 |       mineralWaterTypeId: item.mineralWaterTypeId ?? null,
00791 | 
00792 |       coffeeTypeId: item.coffeeTypeId ?? null,
00793 | 
00794 |       isGift: Boolean(item.isGift),
00795 |       giftReason: item.giftReason?.trim() || null,
00796 |     });
00797 |   }
00798 | 
00799 |   function addConfiguredProduct(item: Omit<ProductCartItem, "localId">) {
00800 |     const newSignature = getCartItemSignature(item);
00801 | 
00802 |     setCart((current) => {
00803 |       const existingIndex = current.findIndex((cartItem) => {
00804 |         if (cartItem.itemType !== "product") {
00805 |           return false;
00806 |         }
00807 | 
00808 |         return getCartItemSignature(cartItem) === newSignature;
00809 |       });
00810 | 
00811 |       if (existingIndex === -1) {
00812 |         const newProductItem: ProductCartItem = {
00813 |           ...item,
00814 |           localId: `${item.product.id}-${Date.now()}-${Math.random()}`,
00815 |         };
00816 | 
00817 |         return [...current, newProductItem];
00818 |       }
00819 | 
00820 |       return current.map((cartItem, index) => {
00821 |         if (index !== existingIndex || cartItem.itemType !== "product") {
00822 |           return cartItem;
00823 |         }
00824 | 
00825 |         return {
00826 |           ...cartItem,
00827 |           quantity: cartItem.quantity + item.quantity,
00828 |         };
00829 |       });
00830 |     });
00831 | 
00832 |     setEditingItem(null);
00833 |     setConfiguringProduct(null);
00834 |   }

00836 |   function imprimirVentaEnSegundoPlano(saleId: number) {
00837 |     const iframe = window.document.createElement("iframe");
00838 | 
00839 |     const printId = `nook-print-${saleId}-${Date.now()}`;
00840 | 
00841 |     iframe.id = printId;
00842 |     iframe.title = "Impresión Nook";
00843 |     iframe.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
00844 | 
00845 |     iframe.src = `/operacion/ventas/ticket/${saleId}` + "?autoPrint=1";
00846 | 
00847 |     /*
00848 |      * No usamos display:none porque algunos navegadores no
00849 |      * imprimen el contenido de elementos completamente ocultos.
00850 |      */
00851 |     Object.assign(iframe.style, {
00852 |       position: "fixed",
00853 |       left: "-10000px",
00854 |       top: "0",
00855 |       width: "80mm",
00856 |       height: "1px",
00857 |       border: "0",
00858 |       opacity: "0",
00859 |       pointerEvents: "none",
00860 |     });
00861 | 
00862 |     let cleaned = false;
00863 | 
00864 |     function cleanup() {
00865 |       if (cleaned) return;
00866 | 
00867 |       cleaned = true;
00868 | 
00869 |       window.clearTimeout(cleanupTimeout);
00870 | 
00871 |       window.removeEventListener("message", handlePrintMessage);
00872 | 
00873 |       iframe.remove();
00874 |     }
00875 | 
00876 |     function handlePrintMessage(event: MessageEvent) {
00877 |       if (event.origin !== window.location.origin) {
00878 |         return;
00879 |       }
00880 | 
00881 |       if (
00882 |         event.data?.type === "NOOK_PRINT_COMPLETED" &&
00883 |         Number(event.data?.saleId) === saleId
00884 |       ) {
00885 |         /*
00886 |          * Damos un margen breve después del diálogo para evitar
00887 |          * retirar el iframe antes de que el driver finalice.
00888 |          */
00889 |         window.setTimeout(cleanup, 500);
00890 |       }
00891 |     }
00892 | 
00893 |     window.addEventListener("message", handlePrintMessage);
00894 | 
00895 |     /*
00896 |      * Contingencia: si el navegador no emite afterprint,
00897 |      * el iframe se elimina igualmente después de un minuto.
00898 |      */
00899 |     const cleanupTimeout = window.setTimeout(cleanup, 60000);
00900 | 
00901 |     window.document.body.appendChild(iframe);
00902 |   }

00904 |   async function confirmarVenta() {
00905 |     if (saving) {
00906 |       return;
00907 |     }
00908 |     const error = validarVenta();
00909 | 
00910 |     if (error) {
00911 |       setMessage(error);
00912 |       return;
00913 |     }
00914 | 
00915 |     try {
00916 |       setSaving(true);
00917 |       setMessage("");
00918 | 
00919 |       const payload = {
00920 |         channel,
00921 |         externalOrderId: channel === "local" ? null : externalOrderId.trim(),
00922 |         paymentMethod,
00923 |         orderNotes: orderNotes.trim() || null,
00924 |         customerId: selectedCliente?.id ?? null,
00925 |         rewardId: selectedReward?.id ?? null,
00926 |         promotionalStamps,
00927 |         promotionReason: promotionalStamps > 0 ? "Promoción RRSS" : null,
00928 |         manualDiscountType: manualDiscountEnabled ? manualDiscountType : null,
00929 |         manualDiscountValue: manualDiscountEnabled
00930 |           ? parsedManualDiscountValue
00931 |           : null,
00932 |         manualDiscountReason: manualDiscountEnabled
00933 |           ? manualDiscountReason
00934 |           : null,
00935 |         manualDiscountNotes: manualDiscountEnabled
00936 |           ? manualDiscountNotes.trim() || null
00937 |           : null,
00938 |         items: cart.map((item) => {
00939 |           if (item.itemType === "custom") {
00940 |             return {
00941 |               item_type: "CUSTOM",
00942 |               custom_name: item.customName.trim(),
00943 |               unit_price: item.customUnitPrice,
00944 |               quantity: item.quantity,
00945 |               loyalty_eligible: item.loyaltyEligible,
00946 |               notes: item.notes.trim() || null,
00947 |               options: [],
00948 |               is_gift: false,
00949 |               gift_reason: null,
00950 |             };
00951 |           }
00952 | 
00953 |           return {
00954 |             item_type: "PRODUCT",
00955 |             product_id: item.product.id,
00956 |             quantity: item.quantity,
00957 |             is_gift: Boolean(item.isGift),
00958 |             gift_reason: item.isGift ? item.giftReason?.trim() || null : null,
00959 |             extra_unit_price: item.extraUnitPrice || 0,
00960 |             notes: [
00961 |               item.serviceFormat
00962 |                 ? `Formato: ${
00963 |                     item.serviceFormat === "ambos"
00964 |                       ? "vaso + barquillo"
00965 |                       : item.serviceFormat
00966 |                   }`
00967 |                 : null,
00968 |               item.includesCookie ? "Con galleta" : null,
00969 |               ...(item.extraLabels || []),
00970 |               item.notes?.trim() || null,
00971 |             ]
00972 |               .filter(Boolean)
00973 |               .join(" · "),
00974 |             options: [
00975 |               ...item.flavorSelections.map((id) => ({
00976 |                 option_group_code: "flavor",
00977 |                 option_value_id: id,
00978 |                 quantity: 1,
00979 |               })),
00980 | 
00981 |               ...(item.brownieVarietyId
00982 |                 ? [
00983 |                     {
00984 |                       option_group_code: "brownie_variety",
00985 |                       option_value_id: item.brownieVarietyId,
00986 |                       quantity: 1,
00987 |                     },
00988 |                   ]
00989 |                 : []),
00990 | 
00991 |               ...(item.mineralWaterTypeId
00992 |                 ? [
00993 |                     {
00994 |                       option_group_code: "mineral_water_type",
00995 |                       option_value_id: item.mineralWaterTypeId,
00996 |                       quantity: 1,
00997 |                     },
00998 |                   ]
00999 |                 : []),
01000 | 
01001 |               ...(item.coffeeTypeId
01002 |                 ? [
01003 |                     {
01004 |                       option_group_code: "coffee_type",
01005 |                       option_value_id: item.coffeeTypeId,
01006 |                       quantity: 1,
01007 |                     },
01008 |                   ]
01009 |                 : []),
01010 | 
01011 |               ...item.toppingIds.map((id) => ({
01012 |                 option_group_code: "topping",
01013 |                 option_value_id: id,
01014 |                 quantity: 1,
01015 |               })),
01016 |             ],
01017 |           };
01018 |         }),
01019 |       };
01020 | 
01021 |       const res = await fetch("/api/operacion/sales", {
01022 |         method: "POST",
01023 |         headers: {
01024 |           "Content-Type": "application/json",
01025 |         },
01026 |         body: JSON.stringify(payload),
01027 |       });
01028 | 
01029 |       const data = await res.json();
01030 | 
01031 |       if (!res.ok) {
01032 |         setMessage(data.message || "No se pudo crear la venta.");
01033 | 
01034 |         return;
01035 |       }
01036 | 
01037 |       const createdSaleId = Number(data.saleId);
01038 | 
01039 |       if (!Number.isInteger(createdSaleId) || createdSaleId <= 0) {
01040 |         setMessage(
01041 |           "La venta fue creada, pero no se pudo identificar para imprimir.",
01042 |         );
01043 | 
01044 |         return;
01045 |       }
01046 | 
01047 |       const displayOrderCode =
01048 |         data.result?.display_order_code ||
01049 |         data.result?.order?.display_order_code ||
01050 |         `Pedido creado`;
01051 | 
01052 |       imprimirVentaEnSegundoPlano(createdSaleId);
01053 | 
01054 |       setCart([]);
01055 |       setOrderNotes("");
01056 |       setSelectedCliente(null);
01057 |       setSelectedRewardId(null);
01058 |       setCashReceived("");
01059 |       setChannel("local");
01060 |       setExternalOrderId("");
01061 |       setClienteSelectorResetKey((current) => current + 1);
01062 |       setPromotionalStamps(0);
01063 |       setManualDiscountEnabled(false);
01064 |       setManualDiscountType("percent");
01065 |       setManualDiscountValue("");
01066 |       setManualDiscountReason("");
01067 |       setManualDiscountNotes("");
01068 | 
01069 |       const warnings = Array.isArray(data.warnings)
01070 |         ? data.warnings.filter(
01071 |             (warning: unknown) => typeof warning === "string" && warning.trim(),
01072 |           )
01073 |         : [];
01074 | 
01075 |       setMessage(
01076 |         warnings.length > 0
01077 |           ? `Venta creada correctamente. Pedido ${displayOrderCode}. ` +
01078 |               `Preparando impresión. Advertencia: ${warnings.join(" ")}`
01079 |           : `Venta creada correctamente. Pedido ${displayOrderCode}. ` +
01080 |               "Preparando impresión.",
01081 |       );
01082 |     } catch (error) {
01083 |       console.error(error);
01084 | 
01085 |       setMessage("Error inesperado al confirmar venta.");
01086 |     } finally {
01087 |       setSaving(false);
01088 |     }
01089 |   }
01090 | 
01091 |   function duplicateItem(item: ProductCartItem) {
01092 |     setCart((current) =>
01093 |       current.map((cartItem) =>
01094 |         cartItem.localId === item.localId
01095 |           ? { ...cartItem, quantity: cartItem.quantity + 1 }
01096 |           : cartItem,
01097 |       ),
01098 |     );
01099 |   }

01101 |   function reconfigureItem(item: ProductCartItem) {
01102 |     setEditingItem(item);
01103 |     setConfiguringProduct(item.product);
01104 |   }
01105 | 
01106 |   function updateConfiguredProduct(
01107 |     localId: string,
01108 |     updatedItem: Omit<ProductCartItem, "localId">,
01109 |   ) {
01110 |     const updatedSignature = getCartItemSignature(updatedItem);
01111 | 
01112 |     setCart((current) => {
01113 |       const itemBeingEdited = current.find((item) => item.localId === localId);
01114 | 
01115 |       if (!itemBeingEdited || itemBeingEdited.itemType !== "product") {
01116 |         return current;
01117 |       }
01118 | 
01119 |       const matchingItem = current.find(
01120 |         (item): item is ProductCartItem =>
01121 |           item.itemType === "product" &&
01122 |           item.localId !== localId &&
01123 |           getCartItemSignature(item) === updatedSignature,
01124 |       );
01125 | 
01126 |       if (!matchingItem) {
01127 |         return current.map((item) => {
01128 |           if (item.localId !== localId) {
01129 |             return item;
01130 |           }
01131 | 
01132 |           const updatedProductItem: ProductCartItem = {
01133 |             ...updatedItem,
01134 |             localId,
01135 |           };
01136 | 
01137 |           return updatedProductItem;
01138 |         });
01139 |       }
01140 | 
01141 |       return current
01142 |         .filter((item) => item.localId !== localId)
01143 |         .map((item) => {
01144 |           if (
01145 |             item.itemType !== "product" ||
01146 |             item.localId !== matchingItem.localId
01147 |           ) {
01148 |             return item;
01149 |           }
01150 | 
01151 |           return {
01152 |             ...item,
01153 |             quantity: item.quantity + updatedItem.quantity,
01154 |           };
01155 |         });
01156 |     });
01157 | 
01158 |     setEditingItem(null);
01159 |     setConfiguringProduct(null);
01160 |   }
01161 | 
01162 |   return (
01163 |     <>
01164 |       <POSLayout
01165 |         title={channel === "local" ? "Venta local" : "Pedido digital"}
01166 |         subtitle={

01168 |             ? "POS Operacional Nook"
01169 |             : "Ingreso manual multicanal"
01170 |         }
01171 |         center={
01172 |           <div
01173 |             className={
01174 |               configuringProduct
01175 |                 ? "grid h-full min-h-0 min-w-0 grid-cols-[minmax(240px,0.9fr)_minmax(300px,1.1fr)] gap-2 overflow-hidden"
01176 |                 : "grid h-full min-h-0 min-w-0 grid-cols-1 gap-2 overflow-hidden"
01177 |             }
01178 |           >
01179 |             <ProductGrid
01180 |               products={filteredProducts}
01181 |               loading={loading}

01186 |               compact={Boolean(configuringProduct)}
01187 |             />
01188 | 
01189 |             {configuringProduct && (
01190 |               <ProductConfigurator

00015 | function getCreatedSaleId(result: unknown): number | null {
00016 |   if (!result) return null;
00017 | 
00018 |   if (Array.isArray(result)) {
00019 |     for (const item of result) {
00020 |       const saleId = getCreatedSaleId(item);
00021 | 
00022 |       if (saleId) return saleId;
00023 |     }
00024 | 
00025 |     return null;
00026 |   }
00027 | 
00028 |   if (typeof result !== "object") {
00029 |     return null;
00030 |   }
00031 | 
00032 |   const record = result as Record<string, unknown>;
00033 | 
00034 |   const directCandidates = [record.sale_id, record.saleId, record.id];
00035 | 
00036 |   for (const candidate of directCandidates) {
00037 |     const parsed = Number(candidate);
00038 | 
00039 |     if (Number.isInteger(parsed) && parsed > 0) {
00040 |       return parsed;
00041 |     }
00042 |   }
00043 | 
00044 |   if (record.sale && typeof record.sale === "object") {
00045 |     return getCreatedSaleId(record.sale);
00046 |   }
00047 | 
00048 |   if (record.result && typeof record.result === "object") {
00049 |     return getCreatedSaleId(record.result);
00050 |   }
00051 | 
00052 |   return null;
00053 | }

00056 |   if (!value) return null;
00057 | 
00058 |   return Array.isArray(value) ? (value[0] ?? null) : value;
00059 | }

00091 | function getItemFlavorIds(item: unknown): number[] {
00092 |   if (!item || typeof item !== "object") {
00093 |     return [];
00094 |   }
00095 | 
00096 |   const options = (item as Record<string, unknown>).options;
00097 |

00099 |     return [];

00110 |           .trim()

00122 | const OPERATIONAL_HISTORY_TIME_ZONE = "America/Santiago";
00123 | const OPERATIONAL_HISTORY_DAYS_BACK = 14;
00124 | 
00125 | function getOperationalHistoryStartIso(now = new Date()): string {
00126 |   /*
00127 |    * Regla funcional:
00128 |    * - día calendario actual en Chile
00129 |    * - más los 14 días calendario completos anteriores
00130 |    * - total: ventana móvil de 15 días calendario
00131 |    *
00132 |    * Ejemplo:
00133 |    * 27/09/2026 -> desde 13/09/2026 00:00 America/Santiago.
00134 |    */
00135 | 
00136 |   const dateParts = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-CA", {
00137 |     timeZone: OPERATIONAL_HISTORY_TIME_ZONE,
00138 |     year: "numeric",
00139 |     month: "2-digit",
00140 |     day: "2-digit",
00141 |   }).formatToParts(now);
00142 | 
00143 |   const getPart = (type: Intl.DateTimeFormatPartTypes) =>
00144 |     dateParts.find((part) => part.type === type)?.value || "";
00145 | 
00146 |   const year = Number(getPart("year"));
00147 |   const month = Number(getPart("month"));
00148 |   const day = Number(getPart("day"));
00149 | 
00150 |   if (
00151 |     !Number.isInteger(year) ||
00152 |     !Number.isInteger(month) ||
00153 |     !Number.isInteger(day)
00154 |   ) {
00155 |     throw new Error("No fue posible calcular la fecha operacional.");
00156 |   }
00157 | 
00158 |   /*
00159 |    * Trabajamos primero como fecha calendario, no como instante,
00160 |    * para retroceder exactamente 14 días independientemente
00161 |    * de cambios de horario de verano/invierno.
00162 |    */
00163 |   const startCalendarDate = new Date(Date.UTC(year, month - 1, day));
00164 | 
00165 |   startCalendarDate.setUTCDate(
00166 |     startCalendarDate.getUTCDate() - OPERATIONAL_HISTORY_DAYS_BACK,
00167 |   );
00168 | 
00169 |   const targetYear = startCalendarDate.getUTCFullYear();
00170 |   const targetMonth = startCalendarDate.getUTCMonth() + 1;
00171 |   const targetDay = startCalendarDate.getUTCDate();
00172 | 
00173 |   /*
00174 |    * Determinamos el offset real de America/Santiago para la fecha
00175 |    * inicial. No fijamos manualmente -03:00 o -04:00.
00176 |    */
00177 |   const offsetProbe = new Date(
00178 |     Date.UTC(targetYear, targetMonth - 1, targetDay, 12, 0, 0),
00179 |   );
00180 | 
00181 |   const offsetParts = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", {
00182 |     timeZone: OPERATIONAL_HISTORY_TIME_ZONE,
00183 |     timeZoneName: "longOffset",
00184 |   }).formatToParts(offsetProbe);
00185 | 
00186 |   const offsetLabel =
00187 |     offsetParts.find((part) => part.type === "timeZoneName")?.value || "";
00188 | 
00189 |   const offsetMatch = offsetLabel.match(/^GMT([+-])(\d{2}):(\d{2})$/);
00190 | 
00191 |   if (!offsetMatch) {
00192 |     throw new Error("No fue posible determinar el huso horario operacional.");
00193 |   }
00194 | 
00195 |   const sign = offsetMatch[1] === "+" ? 1 : -1;
00196 | 
00197 |   const offsetMinutes =
00198 |     sign * (Number(offsetMatch[2]) * 60 + Number(offsetMatch[3]));
00199 | 
00200 |   /*
00201 |    * Convertimos las 00:00 de Chile de la fecha inicial
00202 |    * al instante UTC correspondiente para consultar timestamptz.
00203 |    */
00204 |   const utcTimestamp =
00205 |     Date.UTC(targetYear, targetMonth - 1, targetDay, 0, 0, 0) -
00206 |     offsetMinutes * 60_000;
00207 | 
00208 |   return new Date(utcTimestamp).toISOString();
00209 | }
00210 | export async function GET(req: Request) {
00211 |   const session = await getOperationSession();

00213 |   const authorization = authorizeOperationSession(session, "sales.operate");
00214 | 
00215 |   if (!authorization.ok) {
00216 |     return NextResponse.json(
00217 |       {
00218 |         ok: false,
00219 |         message: authorization.message,

00221 |       { status: authorization.status },
00222 |     );

00225 |   try {

00229 |     const requestedPageSize = Number(searchParams.get("pageSize") || 50);
00230 |

00234 |         : 1;

00237 |       Number.isFinite(requestedPageSize) && requestedPageSize > 0

00242 |     const dateTo = searchParams.get("dateTo");

00246 |      * de la ventana móvil de 15 días calendario.
00247 |      *
00248 |      * Si el usuario selecciona una fecha inicial más reciente,
00249 |      * respetamos ese filtro.
00250 |      */

00264 |

00283 |     /*

00285 |      * - estado del pedido → orders
00286 |      * - búsqueda por cliente → clientes

00288 |      *
00289 |      * Primero obtenemos los IDs de ventas candidatas y luego
00290 |      * aplicamos esa lista a la consulta principal.
00291 |      */
00292 |     let candidateSaleIds: Set<number> | null = null;
00293 | 
00294 |     function intersectCandidateIds(
00295 |       currentIds: Set<number> | null,
00296 |       ids: number[],
00297 |     ): Set<number> {
00298 |       const nextIds = new Set(ids);
00299 | 
00300 |       if (currentIds === null) {
00301 |         return nextIds;
00302 |       }
00303 | 
00304 |       return new Set([...currentIds].filter((id) => nextIds.has(id)));
00305 |     }
00306 | 
00307 |     if (orderStatus) {
00308 |       const { data: statusOrders, error: statusError } = await supabaseAdmin
00309 |         .from("orders")
00310 |         .select("sale_id")
00311 |         .eq("status", orderStatus)
00312 |         .gte("created_at", operationalHistoryStartIso);
00313 | 
00314 |       if (statusError) {
00315 |         return NextResponse.json(
00316 |           { ok: false, message: statusError.message },
00317 |           { status: 500 },
00318 |         );
00319 |       }
00320 | 
00321 |       candidateSaleIds = intersectCandidateIds(
00322 |         candidateSaleIds,
00323 |         (statusOrders || [])
00324 |           .map((order) => Number(order.sale_id))
00325 |           .filter((id) => Number.isFinite(id)),
00326 |       );
00327 |     }

00329 |     if (search) {
00330 |       const safeSearch = search.replace(/[,%()]/g, " ").trim();

00332 | 
00333 |       const { data: directSales, error: directSalesError } = await supabaseAdmin
00334 |         .from("sales")
00335 |         .select("id")
00336 |         .gte("created_at", operationalHistoryStartIso)
00337 |         .or(
00338 |           [
00339 |             `sale_number.ilike.%${safeSearch}%`,
00340 |             `external_order_id.ilike.%${safeSearch}%`,

00343 |           ].join(","),
00344 |         );

00346 |       if (directSalesError) {
00347 |         return NextResponse.json(
00348 |           { ok: false, message: directSalesError.message },
00349 |           { status: 500 },
00350 |         );
00351 |       }
00352 |

00417 |           searchSaleIds.add(Number(sale.id));

00456 |      */
00457 |     if (candidateSaleIds !== null && candidateSaleIds.size === 0) {
00458 |       return NextResponse.json({
00459 |         ok: true,
00460 |         sales: [],
00461 |         pagination: {
00462 |           page: 1,
00463 |           pageSize,
00464 |           total: 0,
00465 |           totalPages: 1,
00466 |           from: 0,
00467 |           to: 0,
00468 |         },
00469 |       });
00470 |     }
00471 | 
00472 |     let query = supabaseAdmin.from("sales").select(
00473 |       `
00474 |         id,
00475 |         sale_number,
00476 |         channel,
00477 |         external_order_id,
00478 |         integration_source,
00479 |         received_at,
00480 |         customer_id,
00481 |         status,
00482 |         subtotal,
00483 |         discount_total,
00484 |         manual_discount_type,
00485 |         manual_discount_value,
00486 |         manual_discount_amount,
00487 |         manual_discount_reason,
00488 |         manual_discount_notes,
00489 |         total,
00490 |         loyalty_eligible_total,
00491 |         payment_status,
00492 |         payment_method,
00493 |         actor_role,
00494 |         confirmed_at,
00495 |         created_at,
00496 |         clientes (
00497 |           id,
00498 |           nombre,
00499 |           correo,
00500 |           telefono
00501 |         ),
00502 |         orders (
00503 |           id,
00504 |           business_date,
00505 |           daily_order_number,
00506 |           display_order_code,
00507 |           status,
00508 |           notes,
00509 |           created_at
00510 |         )
00511 |       `,
00512 |       {
00513 |         count: "exact",
00514 |       },
00515 |     );
00516 | 
00517 |     if (candidateSaleIds !== null) {
00518 |       query = query.in("id", [...candidateSaleIds]);
00519 |     }
00520 | 
00521 |     if (channel) {
00522 |       query = query.eq("channel", channel);
00523 |     }
00524 | 
00525 |     if (paymentMethod) {
00526 |       query = query.eq("payment_method", paymentMethod);
00527 |     }
00528 | 
00529 |     if (customerType === "identified") {
00530 |       query = query.not("customer_id", "is", null);
00531 |     }
00532 | 
00533 |     if (customerType === "counter") {
00534 |       query = query.is("customer_id", null);
00535 |     }
00536 | 
00537 |     if (dateFrom) {
00538 |       const parsedDateFrom = new Date(dateFrom);
00539 | 
00540 |       if (Number.isNaN(parsedDateFrom.getTime())) {
00541 |         return NextResponse.json(
00542 |           { ok: false, message: "Fecha inicial inválida." },
00543 |           { status: 400 },
00544 |         );
00545 |       }
00546 | 
00547 |       query = query.gte("created_at", parsedDateFrom.toISOString());
00548 |     }
00549 | 
00550 |     if (dateTo) {
00551 |       const parsedDateTo = new Date(dateTo);
00552 | 
00553 |       if (Number.isNaN(parsedDateTo.getTime())) {
00554 |         return NextResponse.json(
00555 |           { ok: false, message: "Fecha final inválida." },
00556 |           { status: 400 },
00557 |         );
00558 |       }
00559 | 
00560 |       query = query.lt("created_at", parsedDateTo.toISOString());
00561 |     }
00562 | 
00563 |     const { data, error, count } = await query
00564 |       .order("created_at", { ascending: false })
00565 |       .range(rangeFrom, rangeTo);
00566 | 
00567 |     if (error) {
00568 |       return NextResponse.json(
00569 |         { ok: false, message: error.message },
00570 |         { status: 500 },
00571 |       );
00572 |     }
00573 | 
00574 |     const total = count || 0;
00575 |     const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / pageSize));
00576 | 
00577 |     return NextResponse.json({
00578 |       ok: true,
00579 |       sales: data || [],
00580 |       pagination: {
00581 |         page,
00582 |         pageSize,
00583 |         total,
00584 |         totalPages,
00585 |         from: total === 0 ? 0 : rangeFrom + 1,
00586 |         to: Math.min(rangeFrom + (data?.length || 0), total),
00587 |       },
00588 |     });
00589 |   } catch (error) {
00590 |     console.error("Error cargando historial de ventas:", error);
00591 | 
00592 |     return NextResponse.json(
00593 |       {
00594 |         ok: false,
00595 |         message: "Error inesperado al cargar el historial de ventas.",
00596 |       },
00597 |       { status: 500 },
00598 |     );
00599 |   }
00600 | }
00601 | 
00602 | export async function POST(req: Request) {
00603 |   const requestStartedAt = performance.now();
00604 | 
00605 |   /*
00606 |    * Auth y validación de caja son independientes.
00607 |    *
00608 |    * Las iniciamos en paralelo para evitar sumar ambas latencias
00609 |    * secuencialmente antes de procesar la venta.
00610 |    *
00611 |    * No relajamos ninguna validación:
00612 |    * - Auth sigue validándose mediante getOperationSession().
00613 |    * - RBAC sigue aplicándose antes de procesar la venta.
00614 |    * - La caja OPEN sigue consultándose directamente en BD.
00615 |    * - El RPC sigue recibiendo la sesión de caja validada.
00616 |    */
00617 |   const authStartedAt = performance.now();
00618 | 
00619 |   const sessionPromise = getOperationSession();
00620 | 
00621 |   const cashValidationStartedAt = performance.now();
00622 | 
00623 |   const activeCashSessionPromise = supabaseAdmin
00624 |     .from("cash_register_sessions")
00625 |     .select("id, status")
00626 |     .eq("status", "OPEN")
00627 |     .limit(1)
00628 |     .maybeSingle();
00629 | 
00630 |   const session = await sessionPromise;
00631 |   const authDurationMs = performance.now() - authStartedAt;
00632 | 
00633 |   const authorization = authorizeOperationSession(session, "sales.operate");
00634 | 
00635 |   if (!authorization.ok) {
00636 |     return NextResponse.json(
00637 |       {
00638 |         ok: false,
00639 |         message: authorization.message,
00640 |       },
00641 |       { status: authorization.status },
00642 |     );
00643 |   }
00644 | 
00645 |   try {
00646 |     const body = await req.json();
00647 | 
00648 |     const correlationId = createCorrelationId("sale-create");
00649 | 
00650 |     const { data: activeCashSession, error: activeCashSessionError } =
00651 |       await activeCashSessionPromise;
00652 | 
00653 |     const cashValidationDurationMs =
00654 |       performance.now() - cashValidationStartedAt;
00655 | 
00656 |     if (activeCashSessionError) {
00657 |       console.error(
00658 |         "Error consultando caja abierta antes de crear venta:",
00659 |         activeCashSessionError,
00660 |       );
00661 | 
00662 |       return NextResponse.json(
00663 |         {
00664 |           ok: false,

00666 |         },
00667 |         { status: 500 },
00668 |       );
00669 |     }
00670 | 
00671 |     if (!activeCashSession) {
00672 |       return NextResponse.json(
00673 |         {
00674 |           ok: false,

00676 |         },

00678 |       );

00680 | 
00681 |     const cashRegisterSessionId = Number(activeCashSession.id);
00682 |

00684 |       !Number.isInteger(cashRegisterSessionId) ||
00685 |       cashRegisterSessionId <= 0
00686 |     ) {
00687 |       console.error(

00689 |         activeCashSession,
00690 |       );

00696 |         },
00697 |         { status: 500 },
00698 |       );

00700 | 
00701 |     const orderNotes = String(body.orderNotes || "").trim();
00702 |

00704 |       .trim()
00705 |       .toLowerCase();
00706 | 
00707 |     const externalOrderId = String(body.externalOrderId || "").trim();
00708 | 
00709 |     const allowedChannels = ["local", "shopify", "uber_eats", "rappi"];
00710 |

00915 |

01178 |         {
01179 |           ok: false,
01180 |           message: "Uno o más productos de la venta no son válidos.",
01181 |         },
01182 |         { status: 400 },
01183 |       );
01184 |     }
01185 | 
01186 |     const productIds = [
01187 |       ...new Set(
01188 |         parsedProductIds.filter(
01189 |           (productId): productId is number => productId !== null,
01190 |         ),
01191 |       ),
01192 |     ];
01193 | 
01194 |     const { data: saleProducts, error: saleProductsError } =
01195 |       productIds.length > 0
01196 |         ? await supabaseAdmin
01197 |             .from("products")
01198 |             .select("id, sku, name")
01199 |             .in("id", productIds)
01200 |         : {
01201 |             data: [],
01202 |             error: null,
01203 |           };
01204 | 
01205 |     if (saleProductsError) {
01206 |       console.error(
01207 |         "Error validando productos antes de crear venta:",
01208 |         saleProductsError,
01209 |       );
01210 | 
01211 |       return NextResponse.json(
01212 |         {
01213 |           ok: false,
01214 |           message: "No fue posible validar los productos de la venta.",
01215 |         },
01216 |         { status: 500 },
01217 |       );
01218 |     }
01219 | 
01220 |     const productsById = new Map(
01221 |       (saleProducts || []).map((product) => [Number(product.id), product]),
01222 |     );
01223 | 
01224 |     const armedPotFlavorIds = new Set<number>();
01225 | 
01226 |     for (const item of productItems) {
01227 |       const productId = getItemProductId(item);
01228 | 
01229 |       if (!productId) {
01230 |         continue;
01231 |       }
01232 | 
01233 |       const product = productsById.get(productId);
01234 | 
01235 |       if (!product) {
01236 |         return NextResponse.json(
01237 |           {
01238 |             ok: false,
01239 |             message: "Uno o más productos ya no están disponibles.",
01240 |           },
01241 |           { status: 400 },
01242 |         );
01243 |       }
01244 | 
01245 |       if (product.sku !== "POT-16-ARMADO") {
01246 |         continue;
01247 |       }
01248 | 
01249 |       const flavorIds = getItemFlavorIds(item);
01250 | 
01251 |       for (const flavorId of flavorIds) {
01252 |         armedPotFlavorIds.add(flavorId);
01253 |       }
01254 |     }
01255 | 
01256 |     if (armedPotFlavorIds.size > 0) {
01257 |       const requestedFlavorIds = [...armedPotFlavorIds];
01258 | 
01259 |       const { data: batchItemRows, error: batchItemsError } =
01260 |         await supabaseAdmin
01261 |           .from("inventory_items")
01262 |           .select(
01263 |             `
01264 |           id,
01265 |           option_value_id,
01266 |           catalog_option_values!inventory_items_option_value_id_fkey (
01267 |             id,
01268 |             name
01269 |           )
01270 |         `,
01271 |           )
01272 |           .eq("item_type", "BATCH")
01273 |           .eq("is_active", true)
01274 |           .in("option_value_id", requestedFlavorIds);
01275 |

01277 |         console.error(
01278 |           "Error validando inventario de sabores:",
01279 |           batchItemsError,
01280 |         );
01281 | 
01282 |         return NextResponse.json(
01283 |           {
01284 |             ok: false,
01285 |             message: "No fue posible validar la disponibilidad de los sabores.",
01286 |           },
01287 |           { status: 500 },
01288 |         );
01289 |       }
01290 | 
01291 |       const inventoryItemIds = (batchItemRows || [])
01292 |         .map((row) => Number(row.id))
01293 |         .filter(
01294 |           (inventoryItemId) =>
01295 |             Number.isInteger(inventoryItemId) && inventoryItemId > 0,
01296 |         );
01297 | 
01298 |       const { data: openBatchRows, error: openBatchesError } =
01299 |         inventoryItemIds.length > 0
01300 |           ? await supabaseAdmin
01301 |               .from("inventory_batches")
01302 |               .select("inventory_item_id")
01303 |               .eq("status", "OPEN")
01304 |               .in("inventory_item_id", inventoryItemIds)
01305 |           : {
01306 |               data: [],
01307 |               error: null,
01308 |             };
01309 | 
01310 |       if (openBatchesError) {
01311 |         console.error("Error validando bachas abiertas:", openBatchesError);
01312 | 
01313 |         return NextResponse.json(
01314 |           {
01315 |             ok: false,
01316 |             message: "No fue posible validar las bachas abiertas.",
01317 |           },
01318 |           { status: 500 },
01319 |         );
01320 |       }
01321 |

01323 |         (openBatchRows || []).map((row) => Number(row.inventory_item_id)),
01324 |       );

01327 |       const flavorNamesById = new Map<number, string>();
01328 |

01347 |           optionValueId > 0 &&
01348 |           hasOpenBatch

01351 |         }

01369 |           },
01370 |           { status: 400 },
01371 |         );
01372 |       }

00237 | function formatRepeatedNames(names: string[]) {
00238 |   const counts = names.reduce<Record<string, number>>((acc, name) => {
00239 |     acc[name] = (acc[name] || 0) + 1;
00240 |     return acc;
00241 |   }, {});

00243 |   return Object.entries(counts)
00244 |     .map(([name, count]) => (count > 1 ? `${name} x${count}` : name))
00245 |     .join(" + ");

00250 |     ? item.sale_item_options
00251 |     : [];

00255 |       (option) =>
00256 |         option.option_group_code === "flavor" ||
00257 |         option.option_group_code === "sabor",
00258 |     )
00259 |     .map((option) => option.option_value_name)
00260 |     .filter(Boolean);
00261 | 
00262 |   const toppings = options
00263 |     .filter(
00264 |       (option) =>
00265 |         option.option_group_code === "topping" ||
00266 |         option.option_group_code === "toppings",
00267 |     )
00268 |     .map((option) => option.option_value_name)
00269 |     .filter(Boolean);
00270 | 
00271 |   return {
00272 |     flavors,
00273 |     toppings,
00274 |   };

00343 |   async function cargarVentas(
00344 |     targetPage = page,
00345 |     filterOverrides?: {
00346 |       dateFrom?: string;
00347 |       dateTo?: string;
00348 |       search?: string;
00349 |       channel?: string;
00350 |       paymentMethod?: string;
00351 |       orderStatus?: string;
00352 |       customerType?: string;
00353 |     },
00354 |   ) {
00355 |     try {
00356 |       setLoading(true);
00357 |       setMessage("");
00358 | 
00359 |       const effectiveDateFrom =
00360 |         filterOverrides?.dateFrom !== undefined
00361 |           ? filterOverrides.dateFrom
00362 |           : dateFrom;
00363 | 
00364 |       const effectiveDateTo =
00365 |         filterOverrides?.dateTo !== undefined ? filterOverrides.dateTo : dateTo;
00366 | 
00367 |       const effectiveSearch =
00368 |         filterOverrides?.search !== undefined ? filterOverrides.search : search;
00369 | 
00370 |       const effectiveChannel =
00371 |         filterOverrides?.channel !== undefined
00372 |           ? filterOverrides.channel
00373 |           : channelFilter;
00374 | 
00375 |       const effectivePaymentMethod =
00376 |         filterOverrides?.paymentMethod !== undefined
00377 |           ? filterOverrides.paymentMethod
00378 |           : paymentFilter;
00379 | 
00380 |       const effectiveOrderStatus =
00381 |         filterOverrides?.orderStatus !== undefined
00382 |           ? filterOverrides.orderStatus
00383 |           : statusFilter;
00384 | 
00385 |       const effectiveCustomerType =
00386 |         filterOverrides?.customerType !== undefined
00387 |           ? filterOverrides.customerType
00388 |           : customerFilter;
00389 | 
00390 |       const params = new URLSearchParams({
00391 |         page: String(targetPage),
00392 |         pageSize: String(pageSize),
00393 |       });
00394 | 
00395 |       if (effectiveDateFrom) {
00396 |         const startDate = new Date(`${effectiveDateFrom}T00:00:00`);
00397 | 
00398 |         params.set("dateFrom", startDate.toISOString());
00399 |       }
00400 | 
00401 |       if (effectiveDateTo) {
00402 |         const endDateExclusive = new Date(`${effectiveDateTo}T00:00:00`);
00403 | 
00404 |         endDateExclusive.setDate(endDateExclusive.getDate() + 1);
00405 | 
00406 |         params.set("dateTo", endDateExclusive.toISOString());
00407 |       }
00408 | 
00409 |       if (effectiveSearch.trim()) {
00410 |         params.set("search", effectiveSearch.trim());
00411 |       }
00412 | 
00413 |       if (effectiveChannel !== "all") {
00414 |         params.set("channel", effectiveChannel);
00415 |       }
00416 | 
00417 |       if (effectivePaymentMethod !== "all") {
00418 |         params.set("paymentMethod", effectivePaymentMethod);
00419 |       }
00420 | 
00421 |       if (effectiveOrderStatus !== "all") {
00422 |         params.set("orderStatus", effectiveOrderStatus);
00423 |       }
00424 | 
00425 |       if (effectiveCustomerType !== "all") {
00426 |         params.set("customerType", effectiveCustomerType);
00427 |       }
00428 | 
00429 |       const res = await fetch(`/api/operacion/sales?${params.toString()}`);
00430 | 
00431 |       const data = await res.json();
00432 | 
00433 |       if (!res.ok) {
00434 |         setMessage(data.message || "No se pudo cargar historial.");
00435 |         return;
00436 |       }
00437 | 
00438 |       const nextSales = Array.isArray(data.sales) ? data.sales : [];
00439 | 
00440 |       setSales(nextSales);
00441 | 
00442 |       setSelectedSale(null);
00443 | 
00444 |       setPage(data.pagination?.page || targetPage);
00445 |       setTotalSales(data.pagination?.total || 0);
00446 |       setTotalPages(data.pagination?.totalPages || 1);
00447 |     } catch (error) {
00448 |       console.error(error);
00449 |       setMessage("Error cargando historial.");
00450 |     } finally {
00451 |       setLoading(false);
00452 |     }
00453 |   }

00501 |   async function saveCustomerChange() {

00526 | 
00527 |       if (!res.ok) {
00528 |         setCustomerChangeMessage(
00529 |           data.message || "No se pudo asignar el cliente.",
00530 |         );
00531 |         return;
00532 |       }
00533 | 
00534 |       const updatedCustomer = data.sale?.clientes || null;
00535 |       const updatedCustomerId = data.sale?.customer_id || null;
00536 | 
00537 |       const updateSale = (sale: Sale): Sale => {
00538 |         if (sale.id !== selectedSale.id) return sale;
00539 | 
00540 |         return {
00541 |           ...sale,
00542 |           customer_id: updatedCustomerId,
00543 |           clientes: updatedCustomer,
00544 |         };
00545 |       };
00546 | 
00547 |       setSales((current) => current.map(updateSale));
00548 | 
00549 |       setSelectedSale((current) => (current ? updateSale(current) : current));
00550 | 
00551 |       setCustomerChangeMessage(
00552 |         data.message || "Cliente asignado correctamente.",
00553 |       );
00554 | 
00555 |       setEditingCustomer(false);
00556 |       setCustomerCandidate(null);
00557 |       setCustomerChangeReason("");
00558 |       setCustomerSelectorResetKey((current) => current + 1);
00559 |     } catch (error) {
00560 |       console.error("Error asignando cliente:", error);
00561 |       setCustomerChangeMessage("Error inesperado al asignar el cliente.");
00562 |     } finally {
00563 |       setSavingCustomer(false);
00564 |     }
00565 |   }

00628 |         const dateB = new Date(b.confirmed_at || b.created_at).getTime();
00629 | 
00630 |         comparison = dateA - dateB;

00634 |         comparison = Number(a.total || 0) - Number(b.total || 0);
00635 |       }
00636 | 
00637 |       if (sortField === "customer") {
00638 |         comparison = String(a.clientes?.nombre || "Mostrador").localeCompare(
00639 |           String(b.clientes?.nombre || "Mostrador"),
00640 |           "es",
00641 |         );
00642 |       }
00643 | 
00644 |       if (sortField === "channel") {
00645 |         comparison = getChannelLabel(a.channel).localeCompare(
00646 |           getChannelLabel(b.channel),
00647 |           "es",
00648 |         );
00649 |       }
00650 | 
00651 |       return sortDirection === "asc" ? comparison : -comparison;
00652 |     });
00653 |   }, [sales, sortField, sortDirection]);

00676 |   function imprimirDocumentoEnSegundoPlano({
00677 |     saleId,

00713 |     let cleaned = false;
00714 | 
00715 |     function cleanup() {
00716 |       if (cleaned) return;
00717 | 
00718 |       cleaned = true;
00719 | 
00720 |       window.clearTimeout(cleanupTimeout);
00721 | 
00722 |       window.removeEventListener("message", handlePrintMessage);
00723 |

00725 |     }
00726 | 
00727 |     function handlePrintMessage(event: MessageEvent) {
00728 |       if (event.origin !== window.location.origin) {
00729 |         return;
00730 |       }
00731 | 
00732 |       if (
00733 |         event.data?.type === "NOOK_PRINT_COMPLETED" &&
00734 |         Number(event.data?.saleId) === saleId &&
00735 |         event.data?.documentType === documentType
00736 |       ) {
00737 |         window.setTimeout(cleanup, 500);
00738 |       }
00739 |     }
00740 | 
00741 |     window.addEventListener("message", handlePrintMessage);
00742 | 
00743 |     const cleanupTimeout = window.setTimeout(cleanup, 60000);
00744 | 
00745 |     window.document.body.appendChild(iframe);
00746 |   }

00748 |   async function cancelarPedidoSeleccionado() {
00749 |     if (!selectedSale || !selectedOrder) {
00750 |       setMessage("No se pudo identificar el pedido que deseas anular.");
00751 |       return;
00752 |     }
00753 | 
00754 |     const cancellableStatuses = ["pending", "preparing", "ready"];
00755 | 
00756 |     if (
00757 |       !cancellableStatuses.includes(
00758 |         String(selectedOrder.status || "").toLowerCase(),
00759 |       )
00760 |     ) {
00761 |       setMessage(
00762 |         selectedOrder.status === "delivered"
00763 |           ? "Los pedidos entregados requieren un proceso de devolución y no pueden anularse desde esta acción."
00764 |           : "Este pedido ya no se encuentra disponible para anulación.",
00765 |       );
00766 |       return;
00767 |     }
00768 | 
00769 |     const reason = window.prompt(
00770 |       `Indica el motivo de anulación del pedido ${selectedOrder.display_order_code}:`,
00771 |     );
00772 | 
00773 |     if (reason === null) {
00774 |       return;
00775 |     }
00776 | 
00777 |     const normalizedReason = reason.trim();
00778 | 
00779 |     if (!normalizedReason) {
00780 |       setMessage("Debes ingresar un motivo para anular el pedido.");
00781 |       return;
00782 |     }
00783 | 
00784 |     const confirmed = window.confirm(
00785 |       `¿Confirmas la anulación de ${selectedOrder.display_order_code}?\n\nEsta acción cancelará el pedido y la venta asociada.`,
00786 |     );
00787 | 
00788 |     if (!confirmed) {
00789 |       return;
00790 |     }
00791 | 
00792 |     try {
00793 |       setCancellingOrderId(selectedOrder.id);
00794 |       setMessage("");
00795 | 
00796 |       const res = await fetch("/api/operacion/orders/status", {
00797 |         method: "POST",
00798 |         headers: {
00799 |           "Content-Type": "application/json",
00800 |         },
00801 |         body: JSON.stringify({
00802 |           orderId: selectedOrder.id,
00803 |           newStatus: "cancelled",
00804 |           notes: normalizedReason,
00805 |         }),
00806 |       });
00807 | 
00808 |       const data = await res.json();
00809 | 
00810 |       if (!res.ok) {
00811 |         setMessage(data.message || "No se pudo anular el pedido.");
00812 |         return;
00813 |       }
00814 | 
00815 |       setSelectedSale(null);
00816 | 
00817 |       await cargarVentas(page);
00818 | 
00819 |       setMessage(
00820 |         `Pedido ${selectedOrder.display_order_code} anulado correctamente.`,
00821 |       );
00822 |     } catch (error) {
00823 |       console.error("Error anulando pedido:", error);
00824 | 
00825 |       setMessage("Ocurrió un error inesperado al anular el pedido.");
00826 |     } finally {
00827 |       setCancellingOrderId(null);
00828 |     }
00829 |   }
00830 |

00839 |             >

00279 | const CASH_DENOMINATIONS = [
00280 |   20000, 10000, 5000, 2000, 1000, 500, 100, 50, 10,
00281 | ] as const;
00282 | 
00283 | function createEmptyCashCount(): CashCountQuantities {
00284 |   return Object.fromEntries(
00285 |     CASH_DENOMINATIONS.map((denomination) => [denomination, ""]),
00286 |   ) as CashCountQuantities;
00287 | }
00288 | 
00289 | function cashCountToEntries(quantities: CashCountQuantities): CashCountEntry[] {
00290 |   return CASH_DENOMINATIONS.map((denomination) => {
00291 |     const rawQuantity = quantities[denomination]?.trim() || "";
00292 |     const parsedQuantity = rawQuantity === "" ? 0 : Number(rawQuantity);
00293 | 
00294 |     return {
00295 |       denomination,
00296 |       quantity:
00297 |         Number.isInteger(parsedQuantity) && parsedQuantity >= 0
00298 |           ? parsedQuantity
00299 |           : 0,
00300 |     };
00301 |   });
00302 | }
00303 | 
00304 | function calculateCashCountTotal(quantities: CashCountQuantities): number {
00305 |   return cashCountToEntries(quantities).reduce(
00306 |     (total, entry) => total + entry.denomination * entry.quantity,
00307 |     0,
00308 |   );
00309 | }
00310 | 
00311 | function isValidCashCount(quantities: CashCountQuantities): boolean {
00312 |   return CASH_DENOMINATIONS.every((denomination) => {
00313 |     const rawQuantity = quantities[denomination]?.trim() || "";
00314 | 
00315 |     if (rawQuantity === "") {
00316 |       return true;
00317 |     }
00318 | 
00319 |     const parsedQuantity = Number(rawQuantity);
00320 | 
00321 |     return Number.isInteger(parsedQuantity) && parsedQuantity >= 0;
00322 |   });
00323 | }

00410 | function getChannelLabel(channel?: string | null) {
00411 |   const normalized = String(channel || "local").toLowerCase();
00412 | 
00413 |   if (normalized === "shopify") return "Shopify";
00414 | 
00415 |   if (
00416 |     normalized === "uber" ||
00417 |     normalized === "uber_eats" ||
00418 |     normalized === "ubereats"
00419 |   ) {
00420 |     return "Uber Eats";
00421 |   }
00422 | 
00423 |   if (normalized === "rappi" || normalized === "rapi") {
00424 |     return "Rappi";
00425 |   }
00426 | 
00427 |   if (
00428 |     normalized === "pedidosya" ||
00429 |     normalized === "pedidos_ya" ||
00430 |     normalized === "pedidos ya"
00431 |   ) {
00432 |     return "PedidosYa";
00433 |   }
00434 | 
00435 |   return "Local";
00436 | }

00438 | function getPaymentMethodLabel(value?: string | null) {
00439 |   const normalized = String(value || "").toLowerCase();
00440 | 
00441 |   if (normalized === "efectivo") return "Efectivo";
00442 |   if (normalized === "tarjeta") return "Tarjeta";
00443 |   if (normalized === "transferencia") return "Transferencia";
00444 |   if (normalized === "pago_electronico") return "Pago electrónico";
00445 | 
00446 |   // Valores históricos: se mantienen para ventas anteriores.
00447 |   if (normalized === "debito") return "Débito";
00448 |   if (normalized === "credito") return "Crédito";
00449 |   if (normalized === "manual") return "Plataforma";
00450 | 
00451 |   return value || "—";

00473 | function getManualDiscountReasonLabel(value?: string | null) {
00474 |   const normalized = value?.trim().toLowerCase();
00475 | 
00476 |   if (!normalized) return "—";
00477 | 
00478 |   // Catálogo vigente
00479 |   if (normalized === "courtesy") return "Cortesía comercial";
00480 |   if (normalized === "promotion") return "Promoción";
00481 |   if (normalized === "platform_discount") return "Descuento plataforma";
00482 |   if (normalized === "customer_compensation") {
00483 |     return "Compensación cliente";
00484 |   }
00485 |   if (normalized === "nooklovers") return "NookLovers";
00486 |   if (normalized === "other") return "Otro";
00487 | 
00488 |   // Catálogo histórico
00489 |   if (normalized === "complaint") return "Reclamo cliente";
00490 |   if (normalized === "agreement") return "Convenio";
00491 |   if (normalized === "exceptional_promotion") {
00492 |     return "Promoción excepcional";
00493 |   }
00494 |   if (normalized === "service_error") return "Error en atención";
00495 | 
00496 |   return value?.trim() || "—";
00497 | }

00499 | function formatRepeatedNames(names: string[]) {
00500 |   const counts = names.reduce<Record<string, number>>((acc, name) => {
00501 |     acc[name] = (acc[name] || 0) + 1;
00502 | 
00503 |     return acc;
00504 |   }, {});
00505 | 
00506 |   return Object.entries(counts)
00507 |     .map(([name, count]) => (count > 1 ? `${name} x${count}` : name))
00508 |     .join(" + ");
00509 | }

00511 | function getSaleItemOptions(item: SaleDetailItem) {
00512 |   const options = Array.isArray(item.sale_item_options)
00513 |     ? item.sale_item_options
00514 |     : [];
00515 | 
00516 |   const flavors = options
00517 |     .filter((option) => ["flavor", "sabor"].includes(option.option_group_code))
00518 |     .map((option) => option.option_value_name)
00519 |     .filter(Boolean);
00520 | 
00521 |   const toppings = options
00522 |     .filter((option) =>
00523 |       ["topping", "toppings"].includes(option.option_group_code),
00524 |     )
00525 |     .map((option) => option.option_value_name)
00526 |     .filter(Boolean);
00527 | 
00528 |   const otherOptions = options
00529 |     .filter(
00530 |       (option) =>
00531 |         !["flavor", "sabor", "topping", "toppings"].includes(
00532 |           option.option_group_code,
00533 |         ),
00534 |     )
00535 |     .map((option) => option.option_value_name)
00536 |     .filter(Boolean);
00537 | 
00538 |   return {
00539 |     flavors,
00540 |     toppings,
00541 |     otherOptions,
00542 |   };
00543 | }

00549 | function CashCountForm({
00550 |   title,
00551 |   description,
00552 |   quantities,
00553 |   onQuantityChange,
00554 |   disabled = false,
00555 | }: {
00556 |   title: string;
00557 |   description: string;
00558 |   quantities: CashCountQuantities;
00559 |   onQuantityChange: (denomination: number, value: string) => void;
00560 |   disabled?: boolean;
00561 | }) {
00562 |   const groups = [
00563 |     {
00564 |       title: "Billetes",
00565 |       denominations: CASH_DENOMINATIONS.filter(
00566 |         (denomination) => denomination >= 1000,
00567 |       ),
00568 |     },
00569 |     {
00570 |       title: "Monedas",
00571 |       denominations: CASH_DENOMINATIONS.filter(
00572 |         (denomination) => denomination < 1000,
00573 |       ),
00574 |     },
00575 |   ];
00576 | 
00577 |   return (
00578 |     <div>
00579 |       <div className="mb-3">
00580 |         <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-neutral-800">{title}</p>
00581 | 
00582 |         <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-neutral-500">{description}</p>
00583 |       </div>
00584 | 
00585 |       <div className="grid gap-3 md:grid-cols-2">
00586 |         {groups.map((group) => (
00587 |           <div
00588 |             key={group.title}
00589 |             className="overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-neutral-200 bg-white"
00590 |           >
00591 |             <div className="border-b border-neutral-200 bg-neutral-50 px-3 py-2">
00592 |               <p className="text-xs font-bold text-neutral-800">
00593 |                 {group.title}
00594 |               </p>
00595 |             </div>
00596 | 
00597 |             <div className="grid grid-cols-[1fr_76px_1fr] gap-2 border-b border-neutral-200 bg-neutral-50/60 px-3 py-1.5">
00598 |               <p className="text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.1em] text-neutral-500">
00599 |                 Denominación
00600 |               </p>
00601 | 
00602 |               <p className="text-center text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.1em] text-neutral-500">
00603 |                 Cantidad
00604 |               </p>
00605 | 
00606 |               <p className="text-right text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.1em] text-neutral-500">
00607 |                 Subtotal
00608 |               </p>
00609 |             </div>
00610 | 
00611 |             {group.denominations.map((denomination) => {
00612 |               const rawQuantity = quantities[denomination] || "";
00613 |               const parsedQuantity =
00614 |                 rawQuantity === "" ? 0 : Number(rawQuantity);
00615 | 
00616 |               const subtotal =
00617 |                 Number.isInteger(parsedQuantity) && parsedQuantity >= 0
00618 |                   ? denomination * parsedQuantity
00619 |                   : 0;
00620 | 
00621 |               return (
00622 |                 <div
00623 |                   key={denomination}
00624 |                   className="grid grid-cols-[1fr_76px_1fr] items-center gap-2 border-b border-neutral-100 px-3 py-1.5 last:border-b-0"
00625 |                 >
00626 |                   <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-800">
00627 |                     {formatCurrency(denomination)}
00628 |                   </p>
00629 | 
00630 |                   <input
00631 |                     type="text"
00632 |                     inputMode="numeric"
00633 |                     pattern="[0-9]*"
00634 |                     value={rawQuantity}
00635 |                     onChange={(event) =>
00636 |                       onQuantityChange(denomination, event.target.value)
00637 |                     }
00638 |                     placeholder="0"
00639 |                     disabled={disabled}
00640 |                     aria-label={`Cantidad de ${formatCurrency(denomination)}`}
00641 |                     className="h-8 w-full rounded-lg border border-neutral-200 bg-white px-2 text-center text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-900 outline-none transition focus:border-violet-400 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-violet-100 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:bg-neutral-100"
00642 |                   />
00643 | 
00644 |                   <p className="text-right text-xs font-semibold text-neutral-700">
00645 |                     {formatCurrency(subtotal)}
00646 |                   </p>
00647 |                 </div>
00648 |               );
00649 |             })}
00650 |           </div>
00651 |         ))}
00652 |       </div>
00653 |     </div>
00654 |   );
00655 | }

01003 | 
01004 |       setMessageType("error");
01005 |       setMessage("Ocurrió un error al consultar el estado de la caja.");
01006 |     } finally {
01007 |       setLoading(false);
01008 |     }
01009 |   }, [loadMovements, loadClosingHistory]);
01010 | 
01011 |   useEffect(() => {
01012 |     void loadCashRegister();
01013 |   }, [loadCashRegister]);
01014 | 
01015 |   function selectMovementType(type: CashMovementType) {
01016 |     if (showClosingForm || loadingClosingPreview || submittingClosing) {
01017 |       return;
01018 |     }
01019 | 
01020 |     setMovementType(type);
01021 |     setMovementReason(getDefaultReason(type));
01022 |     setMovementAmount("");
01023 |     setMovementNotes("");
01024 |     setMessage("");
01025 |     setShowMovementForm(true);
01026 |   }
01027 | 
01028 |   function closeMovementForm() {
01029 |     setShowMovementForm(false);
01030 |     setMovementAmount("");
01031 |     setMovementNotes("");
01032 |     setMovementReason(getDefaultReason(movementType));
01033 |   }
01034 | 
01035 |   function updateCashCountQuantity(
01036 |     type: "OPENING" | "CLOSING",
01037 |     denomination: number,
01038 |     value: string,
01039 |   ) {
01040 |     if (value !== "" && !/^\d+$/.test(value)) {
01041 |       return;
01042 |     }
01043 | 
01044 |     if (type === "OPENING") {
01045 |       setOpeningCashCount((current) => ({
01046 |         ...current,
01047 |         [denomination]: value,
01048 |       }));
01049 | 
01050 |       return;
01051 |     }
01052 | 
01053 |     setClosingCashCount((current) => ({
01054 |       ...current,
01055 |       [denomination]: value,
01056 |     }));
01057 | 
01058 |     if (closingPreview) {
01059 |       setClosingPreview(null);
01060 |     }
01061 |   }
01062 | 
01063 |   function resetClosingState() {
01064 |     setShowClosingForm(false);
01065 |     setClosingCashCount(createEmptyCashCount());
01066 |     setClosingNotes("");
01067 |     setClosingPreview(null);
01068 |     setLoadingClosingPreview(false);
01069 |     setSubmittingClosing(false);
01070 |   }
01071 | 
01072 |   function startClosing() {
01073 |     closeMovementForm();
01074 |     setClosingCashCount(createEmptyCashCount());
01075 |     setClosingNotes("");
01076 |     setClosingPreview(null);
01077 |     setMessage("");
01078 |     setShowClosingForm(true);
01079 |   }
01080 | 
01081 |   function cancelClosing() {
01082 |     if (loadingClosingPreview || submittingClosing) {
01083 |       return;
01084 |     }
01085 | 
01086 |     resetClosingState();
01087 |     setMessage("");
01088 |   }

01090 |   async function previewClosing(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
01091 |     event.preventDefault();
01092 | 
01093 |     if (!session) {
01094 |       setMessageType("error");
01095 |       setMessage("No existe una caja abierta para cerrar.");
01096 |       return;
01097 |     }
01098 | 
01099 |     if (!isValidCashCount(closingCashCount)) {
01100 |       setMessageType("error");
01101 |       setMessage(
01102 |         "Las cantidades por denominación deben ser números enteros mayores o iguales a cero.",
01103 |       );
01104 |       return;
01105 |     }
01106 | 
01107 |     const cashCount = cashCountToEntries(closingCashCount);
01108 | 
01109 |     setLoadingClosingPreview(true);
01110 |     setMessage("");
01111 | 
01112 |     try {
01113 |       const response = await fetch("/api/operacion/caja/cierre/preview", {
01114 |         method: "POST",
01115 |         headers: {
01116 |           "Content-Type": "application/json",
01117 |         },
01118 |         body: JSON.stringify({
01119 |           cashCount,
01120 |         }),
01121 |       });
01122 | 
01123 |       const data = (await response.json()) as CashClosingPreviewResponse;
01124 | 
01125 |       if (!response.ok || !data.ok || !data.preview) {
01126 |         setMessageType("error");
01127 |         setMessage(
01128 |           data.message ||
01129 |             "No fue posible calcular la previsualización del cierre.",
01130 |         );
01131 |         return;
01132 |       }
01133 | 
01134 |       if (data.preview.cashRegisterSessionId !== session.id) {
01135 |         setMessageType("error");
01136 |         setMessage(
01137 |           "La sesión de caja cambió mientras se calculaba el cierre. Actualiza la página e inténtalo nuevamente.",
01138 |         );
01139 |         return;
01140 |       }
01141 | 
01142 |       setClosingPreview(data.preview);
01143 |       setMessage("");
01144 |     } catch (error) {
01145 |       console.error("Error calculando previsualización del cierre:", error);
01146 | 
01147 |       setMessageType("error");
01148 |       setMessage("Ocurrió un error inesperado al calcular el cierre de caja.");
01149 |     } finally {
01150 |       setLoadingClosingPreview(false);
01151 |     }
01152 |   }
01153 | 
01154 |   async function confirmClosing() {
01155 |     if (!session) {
01156 |       setMessageType("error");
01157 |       setMessage("No existe una caja abierta para cerrar.");
01158 |       return;
01159 |     }
01160 | 
01161 |     if (!closingPreview) {
01162 |       setMessageType("error");
01163 |       setMessage("Debes revisar el conteo antes de confirmar el cierre.");
01164 |       return;
01165 |     }
01166 | 
01167 |     const normalizedNotes = closingNotes.trim();
01168 | 
01169 |     if (closingPreview.requiresNotes && !normalizedNotes) {
01170 |       setMessageType("error");
01171 |       setMessage(
01172 |         "Debes registrar una observación cuando exista una diferencia de caja.",
01173 |       );
01174 |       return;
01175 |     }
01176 | 
01177 |     if (!isValidCashCount(closingCashCount)) {
01178 |       setMessageType("error");
01179 |       setMessage("La composición del efectivo contado no es válida.");
01180 |       return;
01181 |     }
01182 | 
01183 |     const cashCount = cashCountToEntries(closingCashCount);
01184 | 
01185 |     if (
01186 |       calculateCashCountTotal(closingCashCount) !==
01187 |       closingPreview.countedCashAmount
01188 |     ) {
01189 |       setClosingPreview(null);
01190 |       setMessageType("error");
01191 |       setMessage(
01192 |         "El conteo cambió después de revisar el cierre. Debes revisarlo nuevamente.",
01193 |       );
01194 |       return;
01195 |     }
01196 | 
01197 |     setSubmittingClosing(true);
01198 |     setMessage("");
01199 | 
01200 |     try {
01201 |       const response = await fetch("/api/operacion/caja/cierre", {
01202 |         method: "POST",
01203 |         headers: {
01204 |           "Content-Type": "application/json",
01205 |         },
01206 |         body: JSON.stringify({
01207 |           cashCount,
01208 |           closingNotes: normalizedNotes,
01209 |         }),
01210 |       });
01211 | 
01212 |       const data = (await response.json()) as CashClosingResponse;
01213 | 
01214 |       if (!response.ok || !data.ok || !data.closing) {
01215 |         setMessageType("error");
01216 |         setMessage(data.message || "No fue posible cerrar la caja.");
01217 |         return;
01218 |       }
01219 | 
01220 |       const completedClosingResult = data.closing;
01221 | 
01222 |       setSession(null);
01223 |       setMovements([]);
01224 |       setMovementTotals({
01225 |         cashIn: 0,
01226 |         cashOut: 0,
01227 |         net: 0,
01228 |       });
01229 |       resetClosingState();
01230 | 
01231 |       await loadCashRegister();
01232 | 
01233 |       setCompletedClosing(completedClosingResult);
01234 | 
01235 |       setMessageType("success");
01236 |       setMessage(data.message || "Caja cerrada correctamente.");
01237 |     } catch (error) {
01238 |       console.error("Error cerrando caja:", error);
01239 | 
01240 |       setMessageType("error");
01241 |       setMessage("Ocurrió un error inesperado al cerrar la caja.");
01242 |     } finally {
01243 |       setSubmittingClosing(false);
01244 |     }
01245 |   }
01246 | 
01247 |   function finishCompletedClosing() {
01248 |     setCompletedClosing(null);
01249 |     setMessage("");
01250 |     setOpeningCashCount(createEmptyCashCount());
01251 |     setOpeningNotes("");
01252 |   }
01253 | 
01254 |   function closeClosingDetail() {
01255 |     setSelectedClosingId(null);
01256 |     setClosingDetail(null);
01257 |     setClosingDetailError("");
01258 |     setLoadingClosingDetail(false);
01259 | 
01260 |     setSelectedSaleDetail(null);
01261 |     setSaleDetailError("");
01262 |     setLoadingSaleDetail(false);
01263 |   }
