01265 |   async function openCashRegister(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
01266 |     event.preventDefault();
01267 | 
01268 |     if (!isValidCashCount(openingCashCount)) {
01269 |       setMessageType("error");
01270 |       setMessage(
01271 |         "Las cantidades por denominación deben ser números enteros mayores o iguales a cero.",
01272 |       );
01273 |       return;
01274 |     }
01275 | 
01276 |     const cashCount = cashCountToEntries(openingCashCount);
01277 | 
01278 |     try {
01279 |       setSubmitting(true);
01280 |       setMessage("");
01281 | 
01282 |       const response = await fetch("/api/operacion/caja", {
01283 |         method: "POST",
01284 |         headers: {
01285 |           "Content-Type": "application/json",
01286 |         },
01287 |         body: JSON.stringify({
01288 |           cashCount,
01289 |           openingNotes: openingNotes.trim(),
01290 |         }),
01291 |       });
01292 | 
01293 |       const data = (await response.json()) as CashRegisterResponse;
01294 | 
01295 |       if (!response.ok || !data.ok) {
01296 |         if (data.session) {
01297 |           setSession(data.session);
01298 |         }
01299 | 
01300 |         setMessageType("error");
01301 |         setMessage(data.message || "No fue posible abrir la caja.");
01302 |         return;
01303 |       }
01304 | 
01305 |       setSession(data.session ?? null);
01306 |       setOpeningCashCount(createEmptyCashCount());
01307 |       setOpeningNotes("");
01308 |       setCompletedClosing(null);
01309 |       setMovements([]);
01310 |       setMovementTotals({
01311 |         cashIn: 0,
01312 |         cashOut: 0,
01313 |         net: 0,
01314 |       });
01315 | 
01316 |       setMessageType("success");
01317 |       setMessage(data.message || "Caja abierta correctamente.");
01318 | 
01319 |       await loadMovements();
01320 |     } catch (error) {
01321 |       console.error("Error abriendo caja:", error);
01322 | 
01323 |       setMessageType("error");
01324 |       setMessage("Ocurrió un error inesperado al abrir la caja.");
01325 |     } finally {
01326 |       setSubmitting(false);
01327 |     }
01328 |   }
01329 | 
01330 |   async function registerMovement(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
01331 |     event.preventDefault();
01332 | 
01333 |     if (!session) {
01334 |       setMessageType("error");
01335 |       setMessage("Debes abrir la caja antes de registrar movimientos.");
01336 |       return;
01337 |     }
01338 | 
01339 |     const normalizedAmount = movementAmount.trim();
01340 |     const parsedAmount = Number(normalizedAmount);
01341 |     const normalizedNotes = movementNotes.trim();
01342 | 
01343 |     if (
01344 |       normalizedAmount === "" ||
01345 |       !Number.isInteger(parsedAmount) ||
01346 |       parsedAmount <= 0
01347 |     ) {
01348 |       setMessageType("error");
01349 |       setMessage("El monto debe ser un número entero mayor que cero.");
01350 |       return;
01351 |     }
01352 | 
01353 |     if (movementReason === "OTHER" && !normalizedNotes) {
01354 |       setMessageType("error");
01355 |       setMessage("Debes explicar el motivo del movimiento.");
01356 |       return;
01357 |     }
01358 | 
01359 |     try {
01360 |       setSubmittingMovement(true);
01361 |       setMessage("");
01362 | 
01363 |       const response = await fetch("/api/operacion/caja/movimientos", {
01364 |         method: "POST",
01365 |         headers: {
01366 |           "Content-Type": "application/json",
01367 |         },
01368 |         body: JSON.stringify({
01369 |           movementType,
01370 |           amount: parsedAmount,
01371 |           reason: movementReason,
01372 |           notes: normalizedNotes,
01373 |         }),
01374 |       });
01375 | 
01376 |       const data = (await response.json()) as CashMovementsResponse;
01377 | 
01378 |       if (!response.ok || !data.ok) {
01379 |         setMessageType("error");
01380 |         setMessage(
01381 |           data.message || "No fue posible registrar el movimiento de caja.",
01382 |         );
01383 |         return;
01384 |       }
01385 | 
01386 |       setMovementAmount("");
01387 |       setMovementNotes("");
01388 |       setMovementReason(getDefaultReason(movementType));
01389 |       setShowMovementForm(false);
01390 | 
01391 |       setMessageType("success");
01392 |       setMessage(data.message || "Movimiento registrado correctamente.");
01393 | 
01394 |       await loadMovements();
01395 |     } catch (error) {
01396 |       console.error("Error registrando movimiento:", error);
01397 | 
01398 |       setMessageType("error");
01399 |       setMessage("Ocurrió un error inesperado al registrar el movimiento.");
01400 |     } finally {
01401 |       setSubmittingMovement(false);
01402 |     }
01403 |   }
01404 | 
01405 |   return (
01406 |     <main className="min-h-screen bg-[#F6F3FF] p-3">
01407 |       <div className="mx-auto flex min-h-[calc(100vh-24px)] w-full max-w-[1600px] flex-col">
01408 |         <header className="flex shrink-0 flex-col gap-3 rounded-xl bg-white px-4 py-3 shadow-sm sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
01409 |           <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-3">
01410 |             <Link
01411 |               href="/operacion"
01412 |               className="shrink-0 rounded-lg border border-neutral-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-[12px] font-bold text-neutral-700 transition hover:border-violet-300 hover:bg-violet-50"
01413 |             >
01414 |               ← Operación
01415 |             </Link>
01416 | 
01417 |             <div className="min-w-0">
01418 |               <h1 className="text-lg font-black leading-tight text-neutral-900">
01419 |                 Caja
01420 |               </h1>
01421 | 
01422 |               <p className="text-[11px] text-neutral-500">
01423 |                 Apertura, movimientos y cierre de efectivo.
01424 |               </p>
01425 |             </div>
01426 |           </div>
01427 | 
01428 |           <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-2">
01429 |             <button
01430 |               type="button"
01431 |               onClick={() => void loadCashRegister()}
01432 |               disabled={
01433 |                 loading ||
01434 |                 loadingMovements ||
01435 |                 loadingClosingHistory ||
01436 |                 loadingClosingPreview ||
01437 |                 submittingClosing
01438 |               }
01439 |               className="cursor-pointer rounded-lg border border-neutral-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-[12px] font-bold text-neutral-700 transition hover:border-violet-300 hover:bg-violet-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
01440 |             >
01441 |               {loading || loadingMovements ? "Actualizando..." : "Actualizar"}
01442 |             </button>
01443 | 
01444 |             {session && !showClosingForm && (
01445 |               <button
01446 |                 type="button"
01447 |                 onClick={startClosing}
01448 |                 disabled={
01449 |                   loadingMovements ||
01450 |                   submittingMovement ||

01452 |                   submittingClosing
01453 |                 }

01455 |               >
01456 |                 Cerrar caja
01457 |               </button>
01458 |             )}
01459 |           </div>
01460 |         </header>
01461 |

01464 |             className={`mt-2 shrink-0 rounded-lg border px-3 py-2 text-[12px] font-semibold ${
01465 |               messageType === "success"
01466 |                 ? "border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-800"
01467 |                 : messageType === "error"
01468 |                   ? "border-red-200 bg-red-50 text-red-800"
01469 |                   : "border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 text-amber-800"
01470 |             }`}
01471 |           >
01472 |             {message}
01473 |           </div>
01474 |         )}
01475 | 
01476 |         <div className="mt-2 flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col gap-2">
01477 |           {loading ? (
01478 |             <section className="rounded-xl border border-neutral-200 bg-white p-4 shadow-sm">
01479 |               <p className="text-sm text-neutral-600">
01480 |                 Consultando estado de la caja...
01481 |               </p>
01482 |             </section>
01483 |           ) : session ? (
01484 |             <>
01485 |               <section className="rounded-xl border border-neutral-200 bg-white p-4 shadow-sm">
01486 |                 <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 lg:flex-row lg:items-center lg:justify-between">
01487 |                   <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-3">
01488 |                     <span className="h-2.5 w-2.5 shrink-0 rounded-full bg-emerald-500" />
01489 | 
01490 |                     <div className="min-w-0">
01491 |                       <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-2 gap-y-1">
01492 |                         <h2 className="text-[14px] font-black text-neutral-900">
01493 |                           Caja abierta
01494 |                         </h2>
01495 | 
01496 |                         <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-neutral-400">
01497 |                           Sesión #{session.id}
01498 |                         </span>
01499 |                       </div>
01500 |
